/* =========================================================
   EMPLOYEES
========================================================= */

function openEmployees(){
  /* Danh sách nhân viên: tổng điểm theo từng tháng. */
  const monthMap=new Map();
  reports.forEach(r=>{
    const d=parseDate(r.date);
    if(!d || isNaN(d)) return;
    const key=`${d.getFullYear()}-${String(d.getMonth()+1).padStart(2,'0')}`;
    if(!monthMap.has(key)) monthMap.set(key,{key,year:d.getFullYear(),month:d.getMonth()+1});
  });

  const months=[...monthMap.values()].sort((a,b)=>a.key.localeCompare(b.key));
  const names=[...new Set([...employees,...reports.map(r=>r.employee).filter(Boolean)])]
    .sort((a,b)=>String(a).localeCompare(String(b),'vi'));

  let html=`<div class="employee-score-wrap"><table class="employee-score-table"><thead><tr>
    <th class="employee-score-name">Nhân viên</th>
    ${months.map(m=>`<th class="employee-score-month">${String(m.month).padStart(2,'0')}/${m.year}</th>`).join('')}
  </tr></thead><tbody>`;

  names.forEach(n=>{
    html+=`<tr><td class="employee-score-name"><span class="emp-name ${nameClass(n)}">${esc(n)}</span></td>`;
    months.forEach(m=>{
      const total=reports.filter(r=>r.employee===n).filter(r=>{
        const d=parseDate(r.date);
        return d && !isNaN(d) && d.getFullYear()===m.year && d.getMonth()+1===m.month;
      }).reduce((sum,r)=>sum+completedScore(r),0);
      const monthKey=m.key;
      html+=`<td class="employee-score-month employee-score-clickable"
        title="Bấm để xem Thống kê khối lượng công việc tháng ${String(m.month).padStart(2,'0')}/${m.year}"
        onclick="openWorkloadForMonth('${monthKey}', event)">${total}</td>`;
    });
    html+='</tr>';
  });

  html+='</tbody></table></div>';
  document.getElementById('employeeList').innerHTML=names.length && months.length ? html : '<div class="empty">Chưa có dữ liệu điểm theo tháng</div>';
  openModal('employeeModal');
}

/* =========================================================
   OPEN WORKLOAD FROM EMPLOYEE MONTH SCORE
========================================================= */

function openWorkloadForMonth(monthKey, event){
  if(event) event.stopPropagation();

  const monthSelect=document.getElementById('workloadMonth');
  const weekSelect=document.getElementById('workloadWeek');
  const fromInput=document.getElementById('workloadFrom');
  const toInput=document.getElementById('workloadTo');

  if(!monthSelect){
    toast('⚠️ Không tìm thấy bộ lọc tháng của Thống kê khối lượng công việc.');
    return;
  }

  // Nạp lại danh sách tháng trước khi chọn, để tháng vừa có dữ liệu cũng dùng được.
  populateWorkloadFilters();

  const hasMonth=[...monthSelect.options].some(o=>o.value===monthKey);
  monthSelect.value=hasMonth ? monthKey : 'ALL';

  // Khi đi từ Danh sách nhân viên sang thống kê tháng, bỏ các bộ lọc khác
  // để kết quả đúng toàn bộ dữ liệu của tháng được chọn.
  if(weekSelect) weekSelect.value='ALL';
  if(fromInput) fromInput.value='';
  if(toInput) toInput.value='';

  closeModal('employeeModal');

  openModal('workloadModal');
  renderWorkload();

  const monthLabel=monthKey
    ? monthKey.split('-').reverse().join('/')
    : 'đã chọn';

  toast(`📊 Đã mở Thống kê khối lượng — tháng ${monthLabel}`);
}


function openQuickEmployeeAdd(){
  const input=document.getElementById('quickEmployeeName');
  if(input)input.value='';
  openModal('quickEmployeeModal');
  setTimeout(()=>input?.focus(),80);
}

function submitQuickEmployee(){
  const input=document.getElementById('quickEmployeeName');
  const btn=document.getElementById('quickEmployeeSaveBtn');
  const name=String(input?.value||'').trim();
  if(!name){toast('⚠️ Nhập tên nhân viên');input?.focus();return;}
  if(typeof google==='undefined'||!google.script||!google.script.run){processError('employee-add','Không thể thêm nhân viên','Chưa kết nối Apps Script.');return;}
  if(btn){btn.disabled=true;btn.textContent='⏳ Đang thêm...';}
  processStart('employee-add','Đang thêm nhân viên...',name);
  enqueueWrite(
    'Thêm nhân viên',
    ({success,failure,requestId})=>{
      google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(success).withFailureHandler(failure).addEmployeeQuick(name,requestId);
    },
    result=>{
      if(result&&result.employee&&!employees.includes(result.employee))employees.push(result.employee);
      employees=[...new Set(employees.filter(Boolean))];
      if(result?.dataVersion)dataVersion=String(result.dataVersion);
      syncSnapshot.employees=[...employees];
      populateEmployeeControls();
      const sel=document.getElementById('inputEmployee');
      if(sel)sel.value=result.employee;
      closeModal('quickEmployeeModal');
      if(btn){btn.disabled=false;btn.textContent='➕ Thêm nhân viên';}
      processSuccess('employee-add','Đã thêm nhân viên',result.employee);
      toast('✅ Đã thêm nhân viên');
    },
    e=>{
      if(btn){btn.disabled=false;btn.textContent='➕ Thêm nhân viên';}
      processError('employee-add','Không thể thêm nhân viên',e?.message||'Lỗi không xác định');
    },
    {processId:'employee-add',showProcess:false,maxRetries:10,rpc:{method:'addEmployeeQuick',args:[name,'__REQUEST_ID__']}}
  );
}

function addEmployee(){

 let n=
   document.getElementById(
     'employeeAddName'
   ).value.trim();

 if(!n)return;

 if(!employees.includes(n)){
   employees.push(n);
 }

 document.getElementById(
   'employeeAddName'
 ).value='';

 save();
 renderAll();
 openEmployees();

 toast(
   '✅ Đã thêm nhân viên'
 );

}


function removeEmployee(n){

 if(
   reports.some(
     r=>r.employee===n
   )||
   assignedTasks.some(
     t=>t.forEmployee===n
   )
 ){

   toast(
     'Nhân viên này đang có dữ liệu, không thể xóa khỏi danh sách'
   );

   return;

 }

 employees=
   employees.filter(
     x=>x!==n
   );

 save();

 renderAll();
 openEmployees();

}


/* =========================================================
   LOGIN
========================================================= */


function adminOperationStillRunning(){
  return !!(
    writeQueueRunning ||
    writeQueue.length ||
    saveInFlight ||
    postWriteRefreshInFlight ||
    attachmentUploadInFlight>0 ||
    (typeof quickSavingFields!=='undefined' && quickSavingFields.size>0) ||
    (typeof quickPendingWrites!=='undefined' && quickPendingWrites.size>0)
  );
}

function performAdminLogout(){
  pendingAdminLogout=false;
  loggedIn=false;
  adminPassword='';
  updateLogin();
  toast('🔒 Đã đăng xuất');
}

function tryCompletePendingAdminLogout(){
  if(!pendingAdminLogout || !loggedIn)return false;
  if(adminOperationStillRunning())return false;
  performAdminLogout();
  return true;
}

function toggleLogin(){
  if(loggedIn){
    if(adminOperationStillRunning()){
      pendingAdminLogout=true;
      const btn=document.getElementById('loginBtn');
      if(btn){
        btn.disabled=true;
        btn.textContent='⏳ Chờ lưu xong...';
      }
      toast('⏳ Đã yêu cầu đăng xuất • hệ thống sẽ đăng xuất ngay sau khi các thao tác đang lưu hoàn tất.');
      return;
    }

    performAdminLogout();
    return;
  }

  pendingAdminLogout=false;
  openModal('loginModal');
}

function doLogin(){

 const input=
   document.getElementById(
     'password'
   );

 const err=
   document.getElementById(
     'loginError'
   );

 const p=
   String(
     input?.value||''
   ).trim();


 if(!p){

   err.textContent=
     '❌ Vui lòng nhập mật khẩu';

   return;

 }


 err.textContent=
   '⏳ Đang xác thực...';


 if(
   typeof google==='undefined' ||
   !google.script ||
   !google.script.run
 ){

   err.textContent=
     '❌ Trang này chưa chạy bằng Google Apps Script Web App.';

   return;

 }


 google.script.run

 .withSuccessHandler(ok=>{

   if(ok && ok.ok){

     pendingAdminLogout=false;
     loggedIn=true;
     adminPassword=String(ok.token||'');

     input.value='';
     err.textContent='';

     closeModal(
       'loginModal'
     );

     updateLogin();

     toast(
       '✅ Đăng nhập thành công'
     );

     // Tiếp tục ngay các thao tác ghi đang chờ quyền ADMIN.
     setTimeout(()=>{
       try{ pumpWriteQueue(); }
       catch(e){ console.error('RESUME_WRITE_QUEUE_AFTER_LOGIN',e); }
     },50);

   }else{

     err.textContent=
       '❌ Mật khẩu không đúng';

   }

 })

 .withFailureHandler(e=>{

   console.error(
     'LOGIN_ERROR',
     e
   );

   err.textContent=
     '❌ Lỗi Apps Script: '+
     (e?.message||
      'Không xác thực được');

 })

 .loginUser(p);

}


/* =========================================================
   LOGIN UI
========================================================= */

function updateLogin(){

 const loginBtn=
   document.getElementById(
     'loginBtn'
   );

 loginBtn.textContent=
   loggedIn
   ?'🔓 Đăng xuất'
   :'🔒 Đăng nhập';

 loginBtn.className=
   loggedIn
   ?'btn btn-success'
   :'btn';

 document.getElementById(
   'assignForm'
 ).style.display=
   loggedIn
   ?'block'
   :'none';

 const quickBtn=document.getElementById('adminQuickReviewBtn');
 if(quickBtn){
   quickBtn.style.display=loggedIn?'inline-flex':'none';
   quickBtn.classList.toggle('active',loggedIn&&adminQuickReviewMode);
   quickBtn.textContent=(loggedIn&&adminQuickReviewMode)
     ?'⚡ Đang đánh giá nhanh'
     :'⚡ Đánh giá nhanh';
 }

 if(!loggedIn)adminQuickReviewMode=false;
 document.body.classList.toggle('admin-quick-review',loggedIn&&adminQuickReviewMode);

 renderTasks();
 renderGoals();
 renderAll();


 if(pendingAdminLogout && loggedIn){
   const btn=document.getElementById('loginBtn');
   if(btn){
     btn.disabled=true;
     btn.textContent='⏳ Chờ lưu xong...';
   }
 }else{
   const btn=document.getElementById('loginBtn');
   if(btn)btn.disabled=false;
 }
}

let confirmCallback=null;


function openConfirm(
 message,
 callback
){

 confirmCallback=callback;

   'confirmMessage'
 ).textContent=message;

 openModal(
   'confirmModal'
 );

}


function runConfirm(){

 const cb=confirmCallback;

 confirmCallback=null;

 closeModal(
   'confirmModal'
 );

 if(typeof cb==='function'){
   cb();
 }

}


function closeConfirm(){

 confirmCallback=null;

 closeModal(
   'confirmModal'
 );

}

/* ==========================================================
   NETWORK / DRAFT SAFETY
   Giữ dữ liệu form cơ bản khi mất mạng hoặc người dùng reload.
========================================================= */
function hasQueuedNewReportWrite(){
  try{
    if(writeQueue?.some?.(j=>String(j?.rpc?.method||'')==='createReportWithAttachmentsFast')){
      return true;
    }

    const stored=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(WRITE_QUEUE_STORAGE_KEY)||'[]');
    return Array.isArray(stored) && stored.some(j=>
      String(j?.rpc?.method||'')==='createReportWithAttachmentsFast'
    );
  }catch(_){
    return false;
  }
}

function saveDraftSafely(){
  try{
    const task=String(document.getElementById('inputTask')?.value||'');
    const comment=String(document.getElementById('inputEmployeeComment')?.value||'');

    // Form sạch thì không lưu draft rỗng; đồng thời xóa draft cũ.
    if(!task.trim() && !comment.trim() && pendingNewAttachments.length===0){
      localStorage.removeItem('MPVN_REPORT_DRAFT_V22');
      return;
    }

    const draft={
      employee:document.getElementById('inputEmployee')?.value||'',
      date:document.getElementById('inputDate')?.value||'',
      task,
      comment,
      savedAt:Date.now()
    };
    localStorage.setItem('MPVN_REPORT_DRAFT_V22',JSON.stringify(draft));
  }catch(e){}
}

function restoreDraftSafely(){
  try{
    // Nếu báo cáo cũ đã nằm trong write queue thì queue là nguồn sự thật.
    // Không đưa nội dung trở lại form vì người dùng có thể bấm Gửi lần nữa -> trùng dữ liệu.
    if(hasQueuedNewReportWrite()){
      localStorage.removeItem('MPVN_REPORT_DRAFT_V22');
      return;
    }

    const d=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('MPVN_REPORT_DRAFT_V22')||'null');
    if(!d)return;

    // Draft quá 24 giờ: xóa im lặng.
    if(Date.now()-Number(d.savedAt||0)>86400000){
      localStorage.removeItem('MPVN_REPORT_DRAFT_V22');
      return;
    }

    const task=String(d.task||'').trim();
    const comment=String(d.comment||'').trim();

    if(!task && !comment){
      localStorage.removeItem('MPVN_REPORT_DRAFT_V22');
      return;
    }

    // Nếu báo cáo tương ứng đã có trong dữ liệu hiện tại thì draft là bản cũ đã gửi thành công.
    const alreadySaved=(reports||[]).some(r=>
      String(r.employee||'').trim()===String(d.employee||'').trim() &&
      String(r.date||'').trim()===String(d.date||'').trim() &&
      String(r.task||'').trim()===task
    );

    if(alreadySaved){
      localStorage.removeItem('MPVN_REPORT_DRAFT_V22');
      return;
    }

    const t=document.getElementById('inputTask');
    const c=document.getElementById('inputEmployeeComment');

    if(t)t.value=task;
    if(c)c.value=comment;

    if(d.date && validReportDate(d.date)){
      const date=document.getElementById('inputDate');
      if(date)date.value=d.date;
    }

    if(d.employee){
      const e=document.getElementById('inputEmployee');
      if(e&&[...e.options].some(o=>o.value===d.employee))e.value=d.employee;
    }

    // Chỉ thông báo khi thực sự có một draft CHƯA gửi cần cứu.
    processNotify(
      'draft-restored',
      'warning',
      'Đã khôi phục bản nháp chưa gửi',
      'Nội dung này chưa có trong dữ liệu đã lưu. Kiểm tra lại trước khi gửi.',
      null,
      false
    );
  }catch(e){}
}

function clearDraftSafely(){
  try{localStorage.removeItem('MPVN_REPORT_DRAFT_V22');}catch(e){}
}
['inputEmployee','inputDate','inputTask','inputEmployeeComment'].forEach(id=>{
  document.getElementById(id)?.addEventListener('input',saveDraftSafely);
  document.getElementById(id)?.addEventListener('change',saveDraftSafely);
});

/* =========================================================
   START
========================================================= */

purgeLegacyAttachmentConflictErrors();
loadErrorRecords();
clearStaleNewReportDraftOnFreshLoad();
renderAttachmentEditor('new');
document.addEventListener('paste',globalAttachmentPaste,true);
document.addEventListener('keydown',e=>{
  const modal=document.getElementById('attachmentViewerModal');
  if(!modal?.classList.contains('active'))return;
  if(e.key==='Escape'){e.preventDefault();closeModal('attachmentViewerModal');return;}
  if(e.key==='ArrowLeft'){e.preventDefault();attachmentNavigate(-1);}
  else if(e.key==='ArrowRight'){e.preventDefault();attachmentNavigate(1);}
  else if(e.key==='+'||e.key==='='){e.preventDefault();attachmentZoom(.2);}
  else if(e.key==='-'||e.key==='_'){e.preventDefault();attachmentZoom(-.2);}
  else if(e.key==='0'){e.preventDefault();resetAttachmentView();}
});

document.addEventListener('click',e=>{
  const editOpen=document.getElementById('editModal')?.classList.contains('active');
  if(editOpen)return;

  const row=e.target?.closest?.('tr[data-report-id]');
  const tableArea=e.target?.closest?.('#reportTableWrap,#tableModal');

  // Click ra ngoài bảng => bỏ chọn dòng; Ctrl+V tiếp theo đi vào Nhập báo cáo.
  if(!row && !tableArea){
    selectedReportId='';
    applySelectedReportRow();
  }
},true);
init();

</script>

<script id="mpvn-v88-viewer-cleanup">
(function(){
  function cleanupOldViewerUi(){
    const modal=document.getElementById('attachmentViewerModal');
    if(!modal || modal.dataset.viewerVersion!=='V88') return;
    modal.querySelectorAll('.attachment-view-toolbar,.attachment-view-list').forEach(el=>el.remove());
  }
  document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',cleanupOldViewerUi);
  const oldOpen=window.openReportAttachments;
  if(typeof oldOpen==='function'){
    window.openReportAttachments=function(){
      const out=oldOpen.apply(this,arguments);
      cleanupOldViewerUi();
      return out;
    };
  }
  const oldRender=window.renderAttachmentGalleryItem;
  if(typeof oldRender==='function'){
    window.renderAttachmentGalleryItem=function(){
      const out=oldRender.apply(this,arguments);
      cleanupOldViewerUi();
      return out;
    };
  }
})();
</script>

<script id="mpvn-v97-mobile-controller">
(function(){
  const M={views:new Set(['home','reports','input','extras']),actions:{workload:'openWorkload',errors:'openErrorCenter',history:'loadAllReportsManually',refresh:'refreshAllData',help:'openHelp',admin:'openMobileAdminCenter'}};
  const root=document.documentElement;
  function phone(){
    const ua=String(navigator.userAgent||'');
    const uad=navigator.userAgentData||null;
    const isTablet=/iPad|Tablet|PlayBook|Silk|Kindle|KFAPWI|KFTT|KFTHWI|KFTHWA|KFASWI|KFSOWI|SM-T\d+|SM-X\d+/i.test(ua)||(/Android/i.test(ua)&&!/Mobile/i.test(ua));
    if(isTablet)return false;
    if(uad&&typeof uad.mobile==='boolean')return uad.mobile===true;
    if(/iPhone|iPod|Android.*Mobile|Windows Phone|IEMobile|Opera Mini|webOS|BlackBerry|BB10/i.test(ua))return true;
    const shortest=Math.min(Number(screen.width)||9999,Number(screen.height)||9999);
    const coarse=!!(window.matchMedia&&window.matchMedia('(pointer: coarse)').matches);
    const touch=Number(navigator.maxTouchPoints||0)>0;
    return shortest<600&&coarse&&touch;
  }
  function viewportWidth(){
    const vv=window.visualViewport;
    const w=vv&&Number(vv.width)>0?Number(vv.width):Number(window.innerWidth||document.documentElement.clientWidth||390);
    return Math.max(280,Math.min(w,560));
  }
  function setVar(name,value){root.style.setProperty(name,value)}
  function px(n){return Math.round(n*10)/10+'px'}
  function applyScale(){
    if(!phone()){root.style.removeProperty('--mpvn-phone-scale');return 1}
    const w=viewportWidth();
    /* V105: 390 CSS px = scale 1.0. Mọi kích thước mobile dùng cùng một hệ số theo viewport hiện tại. */
    const READABILITY=1;
    const scale=1;
    setVar('--mpvn-phone-scale',String(scale));
    const V={
      '--m-page-pad':12,'--m-body-font':16,'--m-header-h':60,'--m-header-pad-y':10,'--m-header-pad-x':14,
      '--m-logo-font':26,'--m-clock-font':15,'--m-bell':44,'--m-bell-icon':22,'--m-gap-sm':7,
      '--m-date-font':14,'--m-time-font':38,'--m-home-bottom':20,'--m-grid-gap':10,'--m-tile-h':120,
      '--m-tile-gap':8,'--m-tile-pad-y':14,'--m-tile-pad-x':8,'--m-home-icon':50,'--m-home-icon-radius':15,

      '--m-close-top':6,'--m-viewer-head':56,'--m-viewer-pad-y':7,'--m-viewer-pad-x':8
    };
    for(const [k,base] of Object.entries(V))setVar(k,px(base*scale));
    return scale;
  }
  function call(name){try{const fn=window[name];if(typeof fn!=='function')throw new Error(name+' unavailable');return fn()}catch(err){console.error('[MPVN V97]',name,err);try{window.toast&&window.toast('⚠️ Chức năng chưa sẵn sàng. Hãy tải lại trang.')}catch(_){}}}
  function clock(){const n=new Date(),t=document.getElementById('mpvnMobileTime'),d=document.getElementById('mpvnMobileDate');if(t)t.textContent=n.toLocaleTimeString('vi-VN',{hour:'2-digit',minute:'2-digit'});if(d)d.textContent=n.toLocaleDateString('vi-VN',{weekday:'long',day:'2-digit',month:'2-digit',year:'numeric'})}
  function nav(){const v=document.body?.dataset.mobileView||'home';document.querySelectorAll('#mpvnMobileNav [data-view]').forEach(b=>b.classList.toggle('active',b.dataset.view===v))}
  function bell(){const a=document.querySelector('.header-actions');if(!a)return;let b=document.getElementById('mpvnMobileBell');if(!b){b=document.createElement('button');b.type='button';b.id='mpvnMobileBell';b.textContent='🔔';a.appendChild(b);b.addEventListener('click',()=>call('openErrorCenter'))}}
  M.apply=function(){
    const inputCard=document.getElementById('inputEmployee')?.closest('.card');if(inputCard)inputCard.classList.add('mpvn-mobile-input-card');
    const on=phone();root.classList.toggle('mpvn-phone',on);if(!document.body)return on;
    document.body.classList.toggle('mpvn-phone',on);document.body.classList.remove('mpvn-mobile-pro','mobile-mode','mpvn-phone-ui');root.classList.remove('mpvn-mobile-pro','mobile-mode','mpvn-phone-ui');
    if(on){applyScale();if(!M.views.has(document.body.dataset.mobileView))document.body.dataset.mobileView='home';bell();clock();nav()}
    else{document.body.removeAttribute('data-mobile-view');document.body.classList.remove('filters-open');root.style.removeProperty('--mpvn-phone-scale')}
    return on;
  };
  M.show=function(v){if(!M.apply()||!M.views.has(v))return;document.body.dataset.mobileView=v;document.body.classList.remove('filters-open');nav();if(v==='reports'){try{typeof window.renderTable==='function'&&window.renderTable('reportBody')}catch(e){console.error(e)}}requestAnimationFrame(()=>{try{window.scrollTo(0,0)}catch(_){}})};
  M.action=function(a){if(!M.apply())return;if(a==='filters'){document.body.classList.toggle('filters-open');return}const f=M.actions[a];if(f)call(f)};
  window.MPVNMobile=M;
  function bind(){document.querySelectorAll('[data-mview]').forEach(btn=>{if(btn.dataset.v96bound)return;btn.dataset.v96bound='1';btn.addEventListener('click',e=>{e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();M.show(btn.dataset.mview)})});document.querySelectorAll('[data-maction]').forEach(btn=>{if(btn.dataset.v96bound)return;btn.dataset.v96bound='1';btn.addEventListener('click',e=>{e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();M.action(btn.dataset.maction)})})}
  function refreshLayout(){if(M.apply())applyScale();bind()}
  function boot(){M.apply();bind();clock();M.timer=M.timer||setInterval(clock,30000)}
  if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',boot,{once:true});else boot();
  window.addEventListener('resize',()=>{clearTimeout(M.rt);M.rt=setTimeout(refreshLayout,120)},{passive:true});
  window.addEventListener('orientationchange',()=>setTimeout(refreshLayout,180),{passive:true});
  if(window.visualViewport)window.visualViewport.addEventListener('resize',()=>{clearTimeout(M.vrt);M.vrt=setTimeout(refreshLayout,80)},{passive:true});
})();

<script id="mpvn-v98-mobile-elderly-controller">
(function(){
  function isPhone(){
    try{return !!(document.body&&document.body.classList.contains('mpvn-phone'))}catch(_){return false}
  }
  function ensureDirectMultiPicker(mode){
    const isEdit=mode==='edit';
    const oldId=isEdit?'editAttachmentInput':'newAttachmentInput';
    const zoneId=isEdit?'editAttachmentZone':'newAttachmentZone';
    const old=document.getElementById(oldId),zone=document.getElementById(zoneId);
    if(!old||!zone||zone.dataset.v98Picker==='1')return;
    const actions=zone.querySelector('.attachment-actions');
    if(!actions)return;
    zone.dataset.v98Picker='1';

    // Keep original input for documents. It remains multiple and uses the original handler.
    old.multiple=true;
    old.removeAttribute('capture');

    const imageLabel=document.createElement('label');
    imageLabel.className='mpvn-mobile-file-pick images';
    imageLabel.innerHTML='<span>🖼️</span><span>Chọn nhiều ảnh</span>';
    const imageInput=document.createElement('input');
    imageInput.type='file';
    imageInput.accept='image/*';
    imageInput.multiple=true;
    imageInput.setAttribute('aria-label','Chọn nhiều ảnh');
    imageInput.addEventListener('change',function(){
      try{window.handleAttachmentFiles&&window.handleAttachmentFiles(this.files,mode)}finally{this.value=''}
    });
    imageLabel.appendChild(imageInput);

    const docLabel=document.createElement('label');
    docLabel.className='mpvn-mobile-file-pick docs';
    docLabel.innerHTML='<span>📄</span><span>Chọn tệp khác</span>';
    // Move original input inside a directly tappable label. Direct user activation is more reliable in mobile WebViews.
    old.accept='.pdf,.doc,.docx,.xls,.xlsx,.ppt,.pptx,.txt,.csv,image/*';
    old.multiple=true;
    old.removeAttribute('style');
    docLabel.appendChild(old);

    const hint=document.createElement('div');
    hint.className='mpvn-mobile-file-hint';
    hint.textContent='Có thể chọn nhiều ảnh trong một lần. Nếu máy mở thư viện ảnh, nhấn giữ hoặc dùng nút “Chọn” sau khi đánh dấu nhiều ảnh.';
    actions.appendChild(imageLabel);
    actions.appendChild(docLabel);
    actions.appendChild(hint);
  }

  function enlarge(){
    // V108: disabled legacy V98 double-scaling.
    // V105+ is the only authority for phone scale (390px = 1.0).
    if(!isPhone())return;
    ensureDirectMultiPicker('new');
    ensureDirectMultiPicker('edit');
  }
  function run(){setTimeout(enlarge,0);setTimeout(enlarge,250)}
  if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',run,{once:true});else run();
  window.addEventListener('resize',()=>{clearTimeout(window.__mpvnV98Resize);window.__mpvnV98Resize=setTimeout(enlarge,100)},{passive:true});
  window.addEventListener('orientationchange',()=>setTimeout(enlarge,180),{passive:true});
  if(window.visualViewport)window.visualViewport.addEventListener('resize',()=>{clearTimeout(window.__mpvnV98VV);window.__mpvnV98VV=setTimeout(enlarge,80)},{passive:true});
})();
</script>

<script id="mpvn-v99-mobile-fixes-controller">
(function(){
  function isPhone(){return !!(document.body&&document.body.classList.contains('mpvn-phone'))}
  function tunePicker(mode){
    if(!isPhone())return;
    const zone=document.getElementById(mode==='edit'?'editAttachmentZone':'newAttachmentZone');
    if(!zone)return;
    const imageLabel=zone.querySelector('.mpvn-mobile-file-pick.images');
    const imageInput=imageLabel&&imageLabel.querySelector('input[type=file]');
    if(imageLabel){
      const text=imageLabel.querySelector('span:last-child');
      if(text)text.textContent='Thêm nhiều ảnh';
    }
    if(imageInput){
      imageInput.multiple=true;
      imageInput.removeAttribute('capture');
      imageInput.accept='image/jpeg,image/png,image/webp,image/gif,image/heic,image/heif';
      imageInput.setAttribute('multiple','multiple');
    }
    zone.querySelectorAll('.mpvn-mobile-file-hint').forEach(x=>x.remove());
  }
  function compactEmptyReportCells(){
    if(!isPhone())return;
    document.querySelectorAll('#reportTableWrap tbody>tr').forEach(tr=>{
      [9,10].forEach(n=>{
        const td=tr.children[n-1]; if(!td)return;
        const txt=(td.textContent||'').replace(/\s+/g,' ').trim();
        td.classList.toggle('mpvn-mobile-empty-comment',!txt||txt==='-'||txt==='—');
      });
    });
  }
  function apply(){tunePicker('new');tunePicker('edit');compactEmptyReportCells()}
  const style=document.createElement('style');
  style.textContent='body.mpvn-phone #reportTableWrap td.mpvn-mobile-empty-comment{display:none!important}';
  document.head.appendChild(style);
  if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',()=>setTimeout(apply,300),{once:true});else setTimeout(apply,300);
  const obs=new MutationObserver(()=>{clearTimeout(window.__v99m);window.__v99m=setTimeout(apply,80)});
  document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',()=>{try{obs.observe(document.body,{childList:true,subtree:true})}catch(_){}},{once:true});
})();
</script>

<!-- V103: mobile audit + responsive correction -->
<script id="mpvn-v103-mobile-self-audit">
(function(){
  function audit(){
    if(!document.body || !document.body.classList.contains('mpvn-phone')) return [];
    const vw=document.documentElement.clientWidth, bad=[];
    document.querySelectorAll('body *').forEach(el=>{
      const cs=getComputedStyle(el);
      if(cs.display==='none'||cs.visibility==='hidden'||cs.position==='fixed')return;
      const r=el.getBoundingClientRect();
      if(r.width>0 && (r.right>vw+3 || r.left<-3)) bad.push({tag:el.tagName,id:el.id||'',cls:String(el.className||'').slice(0,80),left:Math.round(r.left),right:Math.round(r.right),width:Math.round(r.width)});
    });
    window.MPVN_MOBILE_AUDIT={viewport:vw,overflow:bad.slice(0,80),count:bad.length};
    if(bad.length) console.warn('[MPVN V103 mobile audit] overflow elements:',bad);
    return bad;
  }
  window.auditMPVNMobile=audit;
  addEventListener('load',()=>setTimeout(audit,1200));
  addEventListener('resize',()=>setTimeout(audit,120));
  document.addEventListener('click',()=>setTimeout(audit,250),true);
})();

;window.auditMPVNMobileGeometry=function(){
  if(!document.body||!document.body.classList.contains('mpvn-phone'))return {};
  const squares=[...document.querySelectorAll('#workloadModal .workload-job-square')];
  const thumbs=[...document.querySelectorAll('#newAttachmentList .attachment-item:has(.attachment-item-preview),#editAttachmentList .attachment-item:has(.attachment-item-preview)')];
  const badSquares=squares.filter(el=>{const r=el.getBoundingClientRect();return Math.abs(r.width-r.height)>1.5}).length;
  const badThumbs=thumbs.filter(el=>{const r=el.getBoundingClientRect();return Math.abs(r.width-r.height)>1.5}).length;
  const result={scale:getComputedStyle(document.documentElement).getPropertyValue('--mpvn-phone-scale').trim(),badSquares,badThumbs,squares:squares.length,thumbs:thumbs.length};
  window.MPVN_MOBILE_GEOMETRY=result;return result;
};
</script>

<script id="mpvn-v106-mobile-cleanup">
(function(){
  function compactReportRows(){
    if(!document.body||!document.body.classList.contains('mpvn-phone'))return;
    document.querySelectorAll('#reportTableWrap tbody>tr').forEach(tr=>{
      const fileCell=tr.children[3];
      if(fileCell){
        const hasThumb=!!fileCell.querySelector('.report-thumb-btn,.report-thumb-more');
        fileCell.classList.toggle('mobile-no-files',!hasThumb);
      }
    });
  }
  const st=document.createElement('style');
  st.textContent='body.mpvn-phone #reportTableWrap .report-attachment-cell.mobile-no-files{font-size:0!important;min-height:0!important;height:0!important;overflow:hidden!important}';
  document.head.appendChild(st);
  if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',()=>setTimeout(compactReportRows,300),{once:true});else setTimeout(compactReportRows,300);
  const obs=new MutationObserver(()=>{clearTimeout(window.__v106compact);window.__v106compact=setTimeout(compactReportRows,50)});
  document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',()=>{try{const t=document.querySelector('#reportTableWrap tbody');if(t)obs.observe(t,{childList:true,subtree:true})}catch(e){}},{once:true});
})();
</script>

<script id="mpvn-v107-attachment-grid-controller">
(function(){
  function markImageItems(root){
    if(!root) return;
    root.querySelectorAll('.attachment-item').forEach(function(item){
      var preview=item.querySelector('.attachment-item-preview');
      if(preview && preview.tagName && preview.tagName.toLowerCase()==='img'){
        item.classList.add('mpvn-image-item');
      }else{
        item.classList.remove('mpvn-image-item');
      }
    });
  }

  function applyAttachmentGrid(){
    if(!document.body || !document.body.classList.contains('mpvn-phone')) return;
    markImageItems(document.getElementById('newAttachmentList'));
    markImageItems(document.getElementById('editAttachmentList'));

    /* Make sure all image picker inputs keep multi-select enabled. */
    document.querySelectorAll(
      '#newAttachmentZone input[type="file"][accept*="image"],' +
      '#editAttachmentZone input[type="file"][accept*="image"],' +
      '.mpvn-mobile-file-pick.images input[type="file"]'
    ).forEach(function(input){
      input.multiple=true;
      input.setAttribute('multiple','multiple');
      input.removeAttribute('capture');
    });
  }

  function schedule(){
    clearTimeout(window.__mpvnV107GridTimer);
    window.__mpvnV107GridTimer=setTimeout(applyAttachmentGrid,40);
  }

  if(document.readyState==='loading'){
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',function(){
      applyAttachmentGrid();
      try{
        var obs=new MutationObserver(schedule);
        obs.observe(document.body,{childList:true,subtree:true});
        window.__mpvnV107GridObserver=obs;
      }catch(e){}
    },{once:true});
  }else{
    applyAttachmentGrid();
    try{
      var obs=new MutationObserver(schedule);
      obs.observe(document.body,{childList:true,subtree:true});
      window.__mpvnV107GridObserver=obs;
    }catch(e){}
  }

  window.addEventListener('resize',schedule);
})();
</script>

}

<script id="mpvn-v108-file-read-audit">
(function(){
  function mark(){
    if(!document.body||!document.body.classList.contains('mpvn-phone'))return;
    [['new',window.pendingNewAttachments],['edit',window.pendingEditAttachments]].forEach(()=>{});
    const groups=[
      {mode:'new',list:typeof pendingNewAttachments!=='undefined'?pendingNewAttachments:[],id:'newAttachmentList'},
      {mode:'edit',list:typeof pendingEditAttachments!=='undefined'?pendingEditAttachments:[],id:'editAttachmentList'}
    ];
    groups.forEach(g=>{
      const box=document.getElementById(g.id);if(!box)return;
      const nodes=[...box.querySelectorAll('.attachment-item')];
      g.list.forEach((item,i)=>{
        const node=nodes[nodes.length-g.list.length+i];
        if(node)node.classList.toggle('mpvn-file-read-error',!!item.snapshotError);
      });
    });
  }
  setInterval(mark,800);
})();
</script>

<script id="mpvn-v112-blocking-progress">
(function(){
  function isPhone(){return !!document.body?.classList.contains('mpvn-phone')}

  function ensureOverlay(){
    let ov=document.getElementById('mpvnBlockingProgress');
    if(ov)return ov;
    ov=document.createElement('div');
    ov.id='mpvnBlockingProgress';
    ov.innerHTML=`
      <div class="mpvn-blocking-card" role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-live="assertive">
        <div class="mpvn-blocking-icon">⏳</div>
        <div class="mpvn-blocking-title">Đang xử lý...</div>
        <div class="mpvn-blocking-detail">Vui lòng chờ.</div>
        <div class="mpvn-blocking-counter"></div>
        <div class="mpvn-blocking-bar"><i></i></div>
        <div class="mpvn-blocking-foot">Không đóng trang hoặc chuyển sang màn hình khác cho tới khi hoàn tất.</div>
      </div>`;
    document.body.appendChild(ov);
    return ov;
  }

  function show(type,title,detail,progress){
    if(!isPhone())return;
    const ov=ensureOverlay();
    ov.className='show '+String(type||'loading');

    const icon=ov.querySelector('.mpvn-blocking-icon');
    const t=ov.querySelector('.mpvn-blocking-title');
    const d=ov.querySelector('.mpvn-blocking-detail');
    const c=ov.querySelector('.mpvn-blocking-counter');
    const bar=ov.querySelector('.mpvn-blocking-bar i');

    icon.textContent=
      type==='success'?'✅':
      type==='error'?'❌':
      type==='warning'?'⚠️':'⏳';
    t.textContent=title||'Đang xử lý...';
    d.textContent=detail||'Vui lòng chờ.';

    let pct=Number(progress);
    if(!Number.isFinite(pct))pct=0;
    pct=Math.max(0,Math.min(100,pct));
    bar.style.width=pct+'%';

    const match=String(detail||'').match(/(\d+)\s*\/\s*(\d+)/);
    c.textContent=match ? `${match[1]} / ${match[2]}` : (progress!=null ? `${Math.round(pct)}%` : '');
  }

  function hide(delay=0){
    const ov=document.getElementById('mpvnBlockingProgress');
    if(!ov)return;
    clearTimeout(ov._hideTimer);
    ov._hideTimer=setTimeout(()=>{
      ov.className='';
    },Math.max(0,delay));
  }

  window.MPVNBlockingProgress={show,hide};

  // V116: center blocking popup is ONLY for attachment preparation/upload.
  function isBlockingAttachmentProcess(id){
    const key=String(id||'');
    return key==='attachment-prepare-new'
      || key==='attachment-prepare-edit'
      || key==='attachment-upload';
  }

  const oldProcessNotify=window.processNotify;
  if(typeof oldProcessNotify==='function'){
    window.processNotify=function(id,type,title,detail='',progress=null,sticky=false){
      const res=oldProcessNotify.apply(this,arguments);

      if(isPhone() && isBlockingAttachmentProcess(id)){
        if(type==='loading'){
          show(type,title,detail,progress);
        }else if(type==='success'){
          show(type,title,detail,progress==null?100:progress);
          hide(2200);
        }else if(type==='error' || type==='warning'){
          show(type,title,detail,progress);
        }
      }
      return res;
    };
  }

  // Explicit helpers for image preparation lifecycle.
  const oldUpdate=window.updateAttachmentPreparationUi;
  if(typeof oldUpdate==='function'){
    window.updateAttachmentPreparationUi=function(mode){
      const summary=oldUpdate.apply(this,arguments);
      if(isPhone() && mode==='new' && summary){
        if(summary.pending>0){
          show(
            'loading',
            'Đang chuẩn bị ảnh',
            `${summary.ready}/${summary.total} ảnh đã sẵn sàng • còn ${summary.pending} ảnh`,
            Math.round((summary.ready/Math.max(1,summary.total))*100)
          );
        }else if(summary.error>0){
          show(
            'error',
            'Có ảnh chưa đọc được',
            `${summary.ready}/${summary.total} ảnh sẵn sàng • ${summary.error} ảnh lỗi`,
            Math.round((summary.ready/Math.max(1,summary.total))*100)
          );
        }else if(summary.total>0){
          show(
            'success',
            'Ảnh đã sẵn sàng',
            `${summary.ready}/${summary.total} ảnh đã chuẩn bị xong`,
            100
          );
          hide(1800);
        }
      }
      return summary;
    };
  }

  // Block accidental navigation/back while processing overlay is active.
  document.addEventListener('click',function(e){
    const ov=document.getElementById('mpvnBlockingProgress');
    if(!ov || !ov.classList.contains('show'))return;

    // Allow no click-through while active. The overlay has no dismiss button.
    if(!ov.contains(e.target)){
      e.preventDefault();
      e.stopPropagation();
    }
  },true);

  window.addEventListener('beforeunload',function(e){
    const ov=document.getElementById('mpvnBlockingProgress');
    if(ov && ov.classList.contains('show')){
      e.preventDefault();
      e.returnValue='';
    }
  });
})();
</script>

<script id="mpvn-v115-mobile-admin-controller">
(function(){
  let movedNode=null, placeholder=null;

  function phone(){
    return !!document.body?.classList.contains('mpvn-phone');
  }

  function allUserScreens(){
    return [
      document.getElementById('mpvnMobileHome'),
      document.getElementById('mobileReportsScreen'),
      document.getElementById('mobileInputScreen'),
      document.getElementById('mobileOtherScreen')
    ].filter(Boolean);
  }

  function syncAdminEntry(){
    const btn=document.getElementById('mpvnMobileAdminBtn');
    const tile=document.querySelector('.mpvn-admin-tile');
    if(btn){
      btn.textContent=window.loggedIn?'🛡️ Quản trị':'🔐 Admin';
      btn.setAttribute('aria-label',window.loggedIn?'Mở Trung tâm quản trị':'Đăng nhập Admin');
    }
    if(tile){
      const b=tile.querySelector('b'), sm=tile.querySelector('small');
      if(b)b.textContent=window.loggedIn?'Trung tâm quản trị':'Đăng nhập Admin';
      if(sm)sm.textContent=window.loggedIn?'Công cụ người hướng dẫn':'Người hướng dẫn / quản trị';
    }
  }

  function showAdminScreen(){
    const admin=document.getElementById('mpvnMobileAdminScreen');
    if(!admin)return;
    allUserScreens().forEach(el=>{
      el.hidden=true;
      el.style.display='none';
    });
    admin.hidden=false;
    admin.style.display='block';
    document.body.classList.add('mpvn-admin-screen-open');
    window.scrollTo({top:0,behavior:'auto'});
  }

  window.closeMobileAdminScreen=function(){
    const admin=document.getElementById('mpvnMobileAdminScreen');
    if(admin){
      admin.hidden=true;
      admin.style.display='none';
    }
    document.body.classList.remove('mpvn-admin-screen-open');
    if(window.MPVNMobile && typeof window.MPVNMobile.show==='function'){
      window.MPVNMobile.show('home');
    }else{
      const home=document.getElementById('mpvnMobileHome');
      if(home){home.hidden=false;home.style.display='block';}
    }
    window.scrollTo({top:0,behavior:'auto'});
  };

  window.openMobileAdminCenter=function(){
    if(!phone()){
      if(typeof toggleLogin==='function')toggleLogin();
      return;
    }
    if(!window.loggedIn){
      openModal('loginModal');
      return;
    }
    syncAdminEntry();
    const state=document.getElementById('mobileAdminState');
    if(state)state.innerHTML='✅ <b>Đã đăng nhập người hướng dẫn</b><span>Chọn chức năng cần quản trị.</span>';
    showAdminScreen();
  };

  function restoreMoved(){
    if(movedNode && placeholder?.parentNode){
      placeholder.parentNode.insertBefore(movedNode,placeholder);
      placeholder.remove();
    }
    movedNode=null;
    placeholder=null;
  }

  function moveCard(card,title){
    restoreMoved();
    if(!card)return;
    placeholder=document.createComment('mpvn-admin-tool-placeholder');
    card.parentNode.insertBefore(placeholder,card);
    movedNode=card;
    document.getElementById('mobileAdminToolTitle').textContent=title;
    document.getElementById('mobileAdminToolHost').appendChild(card);
    openModal('mobileAdminToolModal');
  }

  window.closeMobileAdminTool=function(){
    restoreMoved();
    closeModal('mobileAdminToolModal');
  };

  window.mobileAdminLogout=function(){
    closeMobileAdminTool();
    const admin=document.getElementById('mpvnMobileAdminScreen');
    if(admin){admin.hidden=true;admin.style.display='none';}
    document.body.classList.remove('mpvn-admin-screen-open');
    if(window.loggedIn && typeof toggleLogin==='function')toggleLogin();
    setTimeout(()=>{
      syncAdminEntry();
      if(window.MPVNMobile)window.MPVNMobile.show('home');
    },50);
  };

  function run(tool){
    if(!window.loggedIn){
      openModal('loginModal');
      return;
    }

    if(tool==='quick'){
      if(!window.adminQuickReviewMode && typeof toggleAdminQuickReview==='function')toggleAdminQuickReview();
      if(window.MPVNMobile)window.MPVNMobile.show('reports');
      const admin=document.getElementById('mpvnMobileAdminScreen');
      if(admin){admin.hidden=true;admin.style.display='none';}
      document.body.classList.remove('mpvn-admin-screen-open');
      return;
    }

    if(tool==='assign'){
      const form=document.getElementById('assignForm');
      const card=form?.closest('.card');
      if(form)form.style.display='block';
      moveCard(card,'📌 Giao việc');
      return;
    }

    if(tool==='goals'){
      const goals=document.getElementById('goals');
      const card=goals?.closest('.card');
      const editor=document.getElementById('goalEditor');
      if(editor)editor.style.display='block';
      moveCard(card,'🎯 Mục tiêu tuần');
      return;
    }

    if(tool==='comments'){
      if(window.MPVNMobile)window.MPVNMobile.show('extras');
      const admin=document.getElementById('mpvnMobileAdminScreen');
      if(admin){admin.hidden=true;admin.style.display='none';}
      document.body.classList.remove('mpvn-admin-screen-open');
      setTimeout(()=>{
        const el=document.getElementById('managerComments');
        if(el)el.scrollIntoView({behavior:'smooth',block:'start'});
      },120);
      return;
    }

    if(tool==='employees' && typeof openEmployees==='function')return openEmployees();
    if(tool==='workload' && typeof openWorkload==='function')return openWorkload();
    if(tool==='errors' && typeof openErrorCenter==='function')return openErrorCenter();
    if(tool==='fulltable' && typeof openTableFullscreen==='function')return openTableFullscreen();
  }

  document.addEventListener('click',function(e){
    const b=e.target.closest('[data-admin-tool]');
    if(!b)return;
    e.preventDefault();
    run(b.dataset.adminTool);
  });

  if(document.readyState==='loading'){
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',syncAdminEntry,{once:true});
  }else{
    syncAdminEntry();
  }

  const oldClose=window.closeModal;
  if(typeof oldClose==='function'){
    window.closeModal=function(id){
      if(id==='mobileAdminToolModal')restoreMoved();
      const r=oldClose.apply(this,arguments);
      setTimeout(syncAdminEntry,0);
      return r;
    };
  }
})();
</script>

body.mpvn-phone #mpvnMobileAdminScreen{
  display:none;

  min-height:100%;
  padding:0 14px calc(var(--m-nav-h,90px) + 34px + env(safe-area-inset-bottom));

}

}

}

}

  border:0!important;

<script id="mpvn-v116-notification-guard">
(function(){
  const ATTACHMENT_IDS=new Set([
    'attachment-prepare-new',
    'attachment-prepare-edit',
    'attachment-upload'
  ]);

  const old=window.processNotify;
  if(typeof old!=='function')return;

  window.processNotify=function(id,type,title,detail='',progress=null,sticky=false){
    const result=old.apply(this,arguments);

    // Non-attachment operations never own the center blocker.
    if(document.body?.classList.contains('mpvn-phone') && !ATTACHMENT_IDS.has(String(id||''))){
      const ov=document.getElementById('mpvnBlockingProgress');
      // Do not close a blocker that is actively preparing/uploading attachments.
      // We identify that by its visible title/detail text.
      if(ov && ov.classList.contains('show')){
        const txt=(ov.textContent||'').toLowerCase();
        const attachmentActive=
          txt.includes('chuẩn bị ảnh') ||
          txt.includes('đang tải tệp') ||
          txt.includes('ảnh đã sẵn sàng') ||
          txt.includes('tệp đã tải');
        if(!attachmentActive && window.MPVNBlockingProgress){
          window.MPVNBlockingProgress.hide(0);
        }
      }
    }
    return result;
  };
})();
</script>

<script id="mpvn-v117-header-notification-routing">
(function(){
  const ATTACHMENT_IDS=new Set([
    'attachment-prepare-new',
    'attachment-prepare-edit',
    'attachment-upload'
  ]);

  const old=window.processNotify;
  if(typeof old!=='function')return;

  window.processNotify=function(id,type,title,detail='',progress=null,sticky=false){
    const phone=!!document.body?.classList.contains('mpvn-phone');
    const attachment=ATTACHMENT_IDS.has(String(id||''));

    if(phone && !attachment){
      // Normal mobile task:
      // show only in the header beside date/time.
      if(typeof updateHeaderProcessStatus==='function'){
        updateHeaderProcessStatus(type,title,detail,progress);
      }

      // Remove any legacy lower process-card for this task.
      const oldCard=document.getElementById('process-'+id);
      if(oldCard)oldCard.remove();

      // Never open or keep the center blocker for ordinary tasks.
      const blocker=document.getElementById('mpvnBlockingProgress');
      if(blocker && blocker.classList.contains('show')){
        const txt=(blocker.textContent||'').toLowerCase();
        const attachmentStillActive=
          txt.includes('chuẩn bị ảnh') ||
          txt.includes('đang tải tệp') ||
          txt.includes('đang tải ảnh') ||
          txt.includes('ảnh đã sẵn sàng') ||
          txt.includes('tệp đã tải');
        if(!attachmentStillActive && window.MPVNBlockingProgress){
          window.MPVNBlockingProgress.hide(0);
        }
      }
      return;
    }

    // Desktop and attachment operations keep their existing path.
    return old.apply(this,arguments);
  };
})();
</script>

<script id="mpvn-v200-mobile-controller">
(function(){
  function phone(){return !!document.body?.classList.contains('mpvn-phone')}

  function installHeaderRole(){
    if(!phone())return;
    const header=document.querySelector('.header');
    const btn=document.getElementById('mpvnMobileAdminBtn');
    if(header&&btn&&btn.parentNode!==header)header.appendChild(btn);
    try{
      const admin=(typeof loggedIn!=='undefined'&&!!loggedIn);
      if(btn){
        btn.textContent=admin?'🔓 ADMIN':'🔐 Admin';
        btn.setAttribute('aria-label',admin?'Đang ở chế độ Admin':'Đăng nhập Admin');
      }
    }catch(_){}
  }

  function installEditPickers(){
    if(!phone())return;
    const zone=document.getElementById('editAttachmentZone');
    const actions=zone?.querySelector('.attachment-actions');
    const old=document.getElementById('editAttachmentInput');
    if(!zone||!actions||!old)return;

    if(!document.getElementById('editImageInputV200')){
      const x=document.createElement('input');
      x.id='editImageInputV200';x.type='file';x.accept='image/*';x.multiple=true;x.style.display='none';
      x.addEventListener('change',function(){handleAttachmentFiles(this.files,'edit');this.value=''});
      zone.appendChild(x);
    }
    if(!document.getElementById('editOtherInputV200')){
      const x=document.createElement('input');
      x.id='editOtherInputV200';x.type='file';
      x.accept='.pdf,.doc,.docx,.xls,.xlsx,.ppt,.pptx,.txt,.csv';x.multiple=true;x.style.display='none';
      x.addEventListener('change',function(){handleAttachmentFiles(this.files,'edit');this.value=''});
      zone.appendChild(x);
    }
    actions.innerHTML=`
      <button type="button" class="btn btn-info" onclick="document.getElementById('editImageInputV200').click()">🖼 Chọn nhiều ảnh</button>
      <button type="button" class="btn" onclick="document.getElementById('editOtherInputV200').click()">📄 Chọn tệp khác</button>`;
    old.style.display='none';
  }

  function renderEditGallery(){
    if(!phone())return;
    const box=document.getElementById('editAttachmentList');
    if(!box)return;
    const existing=Array.isArray(editingExistingAttachments)?editingExistingAttachments:[];
    const pending=Array.isArray(pendingEditAttachments)?pendingEditAttachments:[];

    const oldHtml=existing.map(a=>{
      const name=esc(a.name||'Tệp'),id=esc(a.id||'');
      if(attachmentIsImage(a)&&attachmentUrl(a)){
        return `<div class="v200-image-card">
          <button type="button" class="v200-image-open" onclick="openEditAttachmentViewer('${id}')">
            <img src="${esc(attachmentUrl(a))}" alt="${name}">
            <span>👁 Xem ảnh</span>
          </button>
          <button type="button" class="v200-image-remove" onclick="event.stopPropagation();removeExistingEditAttachment('${id}')">×</button>
        </div>`;
      }
      return `<div style="grid-column:1/-1;min-height:68px;padding:10px 58px 10px 12px;position:relative;border:1px solid #d5dde6;border-radius:11px;background:#fff">
        <b>📄 ${name}</b>
        <button type="button" class="v200-image-remove" onclick="event.stopPropagation();removeExistingEditAttachment('${id}')">×</button>
      </div>`;
    }).join('');

    const pendingHtml=pending.map(x=>{
      const name=x.file?.name||x.urlAttachment?.name||'Tệp';
      const key=esc(x.key);
      if(x.previewUrl){
        return `<div class="v200-image-card">
          <button type="button" class="v200-image-open" onclick="previewPendingAttachment('edit','${key}')">
            <img src="${x.previewUrl}" alt="${esc(name)}">
            <span>👁 Xem ảnh</span>
          </button>
          <button type="button" class="v200-image-remove" onclick="event.stopPropagation();removePendingAttachment('edit','${key}')">×</button>
        </div>`;
      }
      return `<div style="grid-column:1/-1;min-height:68px;padding:10px 58px 10px 12px;position:relative;border:1px solid #d5dde6;border-radius:11px;background:#fff">
        <b>📄 ${esc(name)}</b>
        <button type="button" class="v200-image-remove" onclick="event.stopPropagation();removePendingAttachment('edit','${key}')">×</button>
      </div>`;
    }).join('');

    box.innerHTML=oldHtml+pendingHtml||'<div class="small" style="grid-column:1/-1;padding:12px">Chưa có tệp</div>';
  }

  function cleanEmptyComments(){
    if(!phone())return;
    document.querySelectorAll('#reportTableWrap tbody>tr').forEach(row=>{
      [9,10].forEach(n=>{
        const td=row.querySelector(`td:nth-child(${n})`);
        if(!td)return;
        const txt=(td.textContent||'').replace(/[—-]/g,'').trim();
        td.style.display=txt?'':'none';
      });
    });
  }

  function refresh(){
    if(!phone())return;
    installHeaderRole();
    installEditPickers();
    if(document.getElementById('editModal')?.classList.contains('active'))renderEditGallery();
    cleanEmptyComments();
  }

  try{
    const original=window.renderAttachmentEditor;
    if(typeof original==='function'){
      window.renderAttachmentEditor=function(mode){
        const r=original.apply(this,arguments);
        if(mode==='edit'&&phone())setTimeout(renderEditGallery,0);
        return r;
      };
    }
  }catch(_){}

  window.MPVNV200={
    refresh,
    renderEditGallery,
    audit:function(){
      if(!phone())return null;
      const visible=[...document.querySelectorAll('button,input,select,textarea')].filter(x=>x.offsetParent!==null);
      const tooSmall=visible.filter(x=>{
        const r=x.getBoundingClientRect();
        if(x.matches('input,select,textarea'))return r.height<52;
        return r.width<44||r.height<44;
      }).map(x=>{
        const r=x.getBoundingClientRect();
        return {id:x.id,cls:String(x.className),text:(x.textContent||'').trim().slice(0,35),w:Math.round(r.width),h:Math.round(r.height)};
      });
      const overflow=[...document.querySelectorAll('body *')].filter(el=>{
        if(!el.offsetParent)return false;
        const r=el.getBoundingClientRect();
        return r.left<-2||r.right>window.innerWidth+2;
      }).slice(0,40).map(el=>({tag:el.tagName,id:el.id,cls:String(el.className).slice(0,60)}));
      return {
        viewport:{w:window.innerWidth,h:window.innerHeight},
        view:document.body.dataset.mobileView||'',
        tooSmall,
        overflow
      };
    }
  };

  function boot(){setTimeout(refresh,0)}
  if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',boot,{once:true});else boot();
  window.addEventListener('resize',()=>setTimeout(refresh,0),{passive:true});
})();
</script>

<script id="mpvn-v202-final-audit">
(function(){
  function phone(){return !!document.body?.classList.contains('mpvn-phone')}

  function restructureHeader(){
    if(!phone())return;
    const header=document.querySelector('.header');
    const actions=header?.querySelector('.header-actions');
    const status=document.getElementById('loadingStatus');
    const clock=document.getElementById('clock');
    const admin=document.getElementById('mpvnMobileAdminBtn');
    if(!header)return;

    if(status&&status.parentNode!==header)header.appendChild(status);
    if(clock&&clock.parentNode!==header)header.appendChild(clock);
    if(admin&&admin.parentNode!==header)header.appendChild(admin);

    if(actions)actions.style.display='none';
  }

  function intersectsViewport(el){
    if(!el||!el.offsetParent)return false;
    const cs=getComputedStyle(el);
    if(cs.display==='none'||cs.visibility==='hidden'||parseFloat(cs.opacity)===0||cs.pointerEvents==='none')return false;
    const r=el.getBoundingClientRect();
    return r.bottom>0&&r.top<window.innerHeight&&r.right>0&&r.left<window.innerWidth;
  }

  function audit(){
    if(!phone())return null;
    const controls=[...document.querySelectorAll('button,input,select,textarea')].filter(intersectsViewport);
    const tooSmall=controls.filter(x=>{
      const r=x.getBoundingClientRect();
      if(x.matches('input,select,textarea'))return r.height<52;
      return r.width<44||r.height<44;
    }).map(x=>{
      const r=x.getBoundingClientRect();
      return {id:x.id,cls:String(x.className),text:(x.textContent||'').trim().slice(0,30),w:Math.round(r.width),h:Math.round(r.height)};
    });

    const overflow=[...document.querySelectorAll('body *')].filter(el=>{
      if(!el.offsetParent)return false;
      const cs=getComputedStyle(el);
      if(cs.display==='none'||cs.visibility==='hidden')return false;
      const r=el.getBoundingClientRect();
      if(r.bottom<=0||r.top>=window.innerHeight)return false;
      return r.left<-2||r.right>window.innerWidth+2;
    }).slice(0,30).map(el=>({tag:el.tagName,id:el.id,cls:String(el.className).slice(0,50)}));

    const header=document.querySelector('.header')?.getBoundingClientRect();
    const status=document.getElementById('loadingStatus')?.getBoundingClientRect();
    const nav=document.getElementById('mpvnMobileNav')?.getBoundingClientRect();
    return {
      viewport:{w:window.innerWidth,h:window.innerHeight},
      view:document.body.dataset.mobileView||'',
      tooSmall,overflow,
      header:header?{w:Math.round(header.width),h:Math.round(header.height)}:null,
      status:status?{w:Math.round(status.width),h:Math.round(status.height)}:null,
      nav:nav?{w:Math.round(nav.width),h:Math.round(nav.height)}:null,
      scrollable:document.documentElement.scrollHeight>window.innerHeight
    };
  }

  function refresh(){
    restructureHeader();
    try{window.MPVNV200?.refresh?.()}catch(_){}
    restructureHeader();
  }

  window.MPVNV202={refresh,audit};
  if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',refresh,{once:true});else refresh();
  window.addEventListener('resize',refresh,{passive:true});
})();
</script>

<script id="mpvn-v206-device-profile-engine">
(function(){
  function readMetrics(){
    const vv=window.visualViewport;
    const width=Math.round(vv?.width||window.innerWidth||document.documentElement.clientWidth||0);
    const height=Math.round(vv?.height||window.innerHeight||document.documentElement.clientHeight||0);
    const innerW=Math.round(window.innerWidth||0);
    const innerH=Math.round(window.innerHeight||0);
    const dpr=Number(window.devicePixelRatio||1);
    const screenW=Math.round(window.screen?.width||0);
    const screenH=Math.round(window.screen?.height||0);

    let profile='wide';
    if(width<=374)profile='compact';
    else if(width<=414)profile='standard';

    /* Senior mode never shrinks due to short height.
       Width only decides how the layout reflows. */
    const fontScale=profile==='compact'?1.22:(profile==='standard'?1.30:1.34);
    const touchScale=profile==='compact'?1.10:(profile==='standard'?1.16:1.18);

    return {width,height,innerW,innerH,dpr,screenW,screenH,profile,fontScale,touchScale};
  }

  function ensureInfo(){
    let el=document.getElementById('mpvnDeviceProfileInfo');
    if(el)return el;
    const header=document.querySelector('.header');
    if(!header)return null;
    el=document.createElement('div');
    el.id='mpvnDeviceProfileInfo';
    el.setAttribute('role','status');
    el.setAttribute('aria-live','polite');
    header.appendChild(el);
    return el;
  }

  function apply(){
    if(!document.body?.classList.contains('mpvn-phone'))return;
    const m=readMetrics();
    document.body.dataset.phoneProfile=m.profile;
    document.documentElement.style.setProperty('--mpvn-auto-font',String(m.fontScale));
    document.documentElement.style.setProperty('--mpvn-auto-touch',String(m.touchScale));
    document.documentElement.style.setProperty('--mpvn-device-vw',String(m.width));
    document.body.style.setProperty('--mpvn-auto-font',String(m.fontScale));
    document.body.style.setProperty('--mpvn-auto-touch',String(m.touchScale));
    document.body.style.setProperty('--mpvn-device-vw',String(m.width));

    const el=ensureInfo();
    if(el){
      const label=m.profile==='compact'?'Điện thoại nhỏ':m.profile==='standard'?'Điện thoại tiêu chuẩn':'Điện thoại lớn';
      el.textContent=`📱 ${m.width}×${m.height} CSS px · DPR ${m.dpr.toFixed(2)} · ${label} · Senior ${m.fontScale.toFixed(2)}×`;
    }

    window.MPVNDeviceProfile=m;
  }

  let timer=0;
  function schedule(){
    clearTimeout(timer);
    timer=setTimeout(apply,60);
  }

  window.MPVNAutoProfile={read:readMetrics,apply};
  if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',apply,{once:true});else apply();
  window.addEventListener('resize',schedule,{passive:true});
  window.addEventListener('orientationchange',schedule,{passive:true});
  window.visualViewport?.addEventListener('resize',schedule,{passive:true});
})();
</script>

<script id="mpvn-v208-wide-layout-phone-engine">
(function(){
  function isLikelyPhone(m){
    const ua=navigator.userAgent||'';
    const mobileUA=/Android|iPhone|iPod|Mobile/i.test(ua);
    const touch=(navigator.maxTouchPoints||0)>0;
    return mobileUA || (touch && m.dpr>=2);
  }

  function measure(){
    const vv=window.visualViewport;
    const width=Math.round(vv?.width||window.innerWidth||document.documentElement.clientWidth||0);
    const height=Math.round(vv?.height||window.innerHeight||document.documentElement.clientHeight||0);
    const dpr=Number(window.devicePixelRatio||1);
    const screenW=Math.round(window.screen?.width||0);
    const screenH=Math.round(window.screen?.height||0);
    return {width,height,dpr,screenW,screenH};
  }

  function apply(){
    if(!document.body?.classList.contains('mpvn-phone'))return;
    const m=measure();
    const phone=isLikelyPhone(m);

    /* Some Android browsers/webviews expose ~980 CSS px even on a phone.
       In that case compensate against a 390 CSS px senior-phone reference. */
    const wideLayoutPhone=phone && m.width>=700 && m.dpr>=2;
    const refWidth=wideLayoutPhone ? 390 : Math.min(430,Math.max(360,m.width||390));
    const layoutComp=wideLayoutPhone ? Math.min(2.70,Math.max(1,m.width/refWidth)) : 1;

    document.body.dataset.wideLayoutPhone=wideLayoutPhone?'true':'false';
    document.body.style.setProperty('--mpvn-layout-comp',String(layoutComp));
    document.documentElement.style.setProperty('--mpvn-layout-comp',String(layoutComp));

    const profile=wideLayoutPhone ? 'standard' : (m.width<=374?'compact':m.width<=414?'standard':'wide');
    document.body.dataset.phoneProfile=profile;

    const info=document.getElementById('mpvnDeviceProfileInfo');
    if(info){
      info.textContent=wideLayoutPhone
        ? `📱 Raw ${m.width}×${m.height} CSS px · DPR ${m.dpr.toFixed(2)} · quy đổi ${refWidth}px · UI ${layoutComp.toFixed(2)}×`
        : `📱 ${m.width}×${m.height} CSS px · DPR ${m.dpr.toFixed(2)} · ${profile}`;
    }

    window.MPVNDeviceProfileV208={...m,phone,wideLayoutPhone,refWidth,layoutComp,profile};
  }

  let t=0;
  function schedule(){clearTimeout(t);t=setTimeout(apply,80)}
  window.MPVNAutoProfileV208={apply,measure};
  if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',apply,{once:true});else apply();
  window.addEventListener('resize',schedule,{passive:true});
  window.addEventListener('orientationchange',schedule,{passive:true});
  window.visualViewport?.addEventListener('resize',schedule,{passive:true});
})();
</script>

<script id="mpvn-v212-balanced-engine">
(function(){
  function apply(){
    if(!document.body?.classList.contains('mpvn-phone'))return;
    const m=window.MPVNDeviceProfileV208;
    if(m&&m.wideLayoutPhone){
      document.body.style.setProperty('--mpvn-layout-comp','1.72');
      document.documentElement.style.setProperty('--mpvn-layout-comp','1.72');
      const el=document.getElementById('mpvnDeviceProfileInfo');
      if(el)el.textContent=`📱 ${m.width}×${m.height} CSS px · DPR ${m.dpr.toFixed(2)} · Senior Balanced`;
    }
  }
  window.MPVNBalancedV212={apply};
  if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',()=>setTimeout(apply,0),{once:true});else setTimeout(apply,0);
  window.addEventListener('resize',()=>setTimeout(apply,80),{passive:true});
})();
</script>
